 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
 632:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
 633: 
 634:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 635:                     try {
 636:                         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 637:                             INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 638:                                 (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 639:                                  generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
 640:                                  execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
 641:                                  effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
 642:                                  confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
 643:                                  trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 644:                             VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
 645:                             """,
 646:                             runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 647:                             Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
 648:                             generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
 649:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 650:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 651:                             generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),
 652:                             generated.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), generated.getPrimaryBlockingStage(),
 653:                             generated.getDetectedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getDetectedRegime().name(),
 654:                             generated.getCandidateRegime() == null ? null : generated.getCandidateRegime().name(),
 655:                             generated.getConfirmedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getConfirmedRegime().name(),
 656:                             generated.getRegimeCandidateCount(), generated.getEntryAuthority(),
 657:                             generated.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), generated.getTrendScore(),
 658:                             generated.getVolumeScore(), generated.getMomentumScore());
 659:                     } finally {
 660:                         profiler.recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 661:                     }
 662: 
 663:                     // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
 664:                     // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while
 665:                     // trade_signal_test is the canonical parity table used for field-by-field comparison.
 666:                     // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
 667:                     // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only
 668:                     // signal remains NULL. This metadata lookup cannot alter Production.
 669:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 670:                     Long sourceSignalId;
 671:                     try {
 672:                         sourceSignalId = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 673:                                 ? replayDataset.sourceSignalId(
 674:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 675:                                 : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 676:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 677:                                         .map(TradeSignal::getId).orElse(null);
 678:                     } finally {
 679:                         profiler.recordLineageLookup(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 680:                     }
 681:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 682:                     try {
 683:                         persistTradeSignalTest(runId, generated, sourceSignalId, null);
 684:                     } finally {
 685:                         profiler.recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 686:                     }
 687:                 }
 688:             } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
 689:                 throw stop;
 690:             } catch (Exception exception) {
 691:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 692:                     errors++;
 693:                     saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle, errorDetail(exception, 900));
 694:                 }
 695:                 log.warn("Regression fresh-signal generation failed: run={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candle={}",
 696:                         runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), exception);
 697:             }
 698: 
 699:             if (index % 10 == 0 && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 700:                 int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());
 701:                 long progressStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 702:                 try {
 703:                     updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", Math.min(73, progress),
 704:                             "Generating fresh signal " + (index + 1) + "/" + timeline.size(), null);
 705:                 } finally {
 706:                     profiler.recordProgressUpdate(System.nanoTime() - progressStartedNs);
 707:                 }
 708:             }
 709:         }
 710: 
  70: 
  71:         TradeSignal latest1m = null;
  72:         TradeSignal latest5m = null;
  73:         TradeSignal latest1h = null;
  74:         ShadowPosition open = null;
  75:         BigDecimal cash = INITIAL_CAPITAL;
  76:         BigDecimal realized = BigDecimal.ZERO;
  77:         int trades = 0, wins = 0, losses = 0;
  78:         int consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = 0;
  79:         WalletSettings replayWalletSettings = walletSettings();
  80:         BigDecimal replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
  81:                 cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
  82:                 replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
  83:         LocalDate replayTradeDate = null;
  84:         int replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
  85:         // FIX-052: when Production live-price events exist, Replay uses them as the
  86:         // authoritative mechanical-protection clock. Older windows created before V64
  87:         // transparently retain candle/signal-close fallback and are therefore not claimed
  88:         // as tick-exact parity runs.
  89:         List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> livePrices = productionPriceEvents == null
  90:                 ? List.of()
  91:                 : productionPriceEvents.stream()
  92:                     .filter(e -> e != null && e.observedAt() != null && e.price() != null)
  93:                     .sorted(Comparator.comparing(MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent::observedAt))
  94:                     .toList();
  95:         boolean exactPriceReplay = !livePrices.isEmpty();
  96:         int livePriceIndex = 0;
  97:         // FIX-109: normal Regression/Replay runs are Production-Parity by default.
  98:         // Experimental rules require an explicitly opened EXPERIMENTAL scope.
  99:         String replayLogicMode = ExecutionReplayScope.ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY.name();
 100:         defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunStart(runId, symbol);
 101:         oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.startRun(runId, symbol);
 102: 
 103:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, timeline,
 104:                 opportunity -> {
 105:                     TradeSignal latest = opportunity == null ? null : opportunity.getLatestSignal();
 106:                     if (latest != null && latest.getGeneratedAt() != null
 107:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
 108:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
 109:                         persistProductionOpportunity(runId, opportunity);
 110:                     }
 111:                 })) {
 112:         // FIX-122: independent OLD comparison revision; ordinary Replay defaults to NEW.
 113:         java.util.List<Boolean> revisions = jdbcTemplate.query(
 114:                 "SELECT enabled FROM fix122_replay_revision WHERE test_run_id=?",
 115:                 (rs,row) -> rs.getBoolean(1), runId);
 116:         replayScope.fix122Enabled(revisions == null || revisions.isEmpty() || revisions.get(0));
 117:         for (TradeSignal signal : timeline) {
 118:             // FIX-090: shadow execution is frequently cancellable without affecting Production.
 119:             if (stopRequested != null && stopRequested.getAsBoolean()) {
 120:                 throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
 121:             }
 122:             // FIX-052 exact ordering: BinanceKlineService invokes live position protection
 123:             // from the 1m price update BEFORE publishing the candle-close analysis event.
 124:             // Consume all persisted Production prices up to this signal timestamp before
 125:             // making the freshly generated signal visible to position management.
 126:             while (livePriceIndex < livePrices.size()
 127:                     && (stopRequested == null || !stopRequested.getAsBoolean())
 128:                     && !livePrices.get(livePriceIndex).observedAt().isAfter(signal.getGeneratedAt())) {
 129:                 MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent live = livePrices.get(livePriceIndex++);
 130:                 replayScope.referencePrice(symbol, live.observedAt(), live.price());
 131:                 // FIX-11Q: advance only the isolated counterfactual grace position. This never mutates
 132:                 // the baseline Shadow position/cash/trades and therefore cannot change Replay parity.
 133:                 oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 134:                 if (open != null && !live.observedAt().isBefore(executionStart) && !live.observedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
 135:                     LivePriceEvaluation protection = evaluateLivePrice(runId, symbol, open, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 136:                     open = protection.position();
 137:                     if (protection.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
 138:                         cash = cash.add(protection.partialProceeds(), MC);
 139:                         realized = realized.add(protection.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
 140:                     }
 141:                     ExitDecision liveExit = protection.decision();
 142:                     if (liveExit.exit()) {
 143:                         BigDecimal proceeds = live.price().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
 144:                         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
 145:                         BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), live.price());
 146:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
 147:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
 148:                         cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
 149:                         realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
 150:                         trades++;
 151:                         if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
 152:                         // wallet_execution_test stores this terminal leg; wallet_position_test stores
 153:                         // the whole lifecycle including any earlier FIX-11T partial realization.
 154:                         persistSellAtPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, open, liveExit, pnl, pnlPct);
 155:                         closePositionAtPrice(runId, open.positionId(), live, liveExit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
 156:                         replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), live.observedAt(), liveExit.reason());
 157:                         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, latest1m, liveExit.reason());
 158:                                                 open = null;
 159:                     }
 160:                 }
 161:             }
 162: 
 163:             replayScope.reference(signal.getGeneratedAt());
 164:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 165:                 latest1m = signal;
 166:                 consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
 167:                         ? consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells + 1 : 0;
 168:             }
 169:             if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest5m = signal;
 170:             if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest1h = signal;
 171:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onSignal(runId, signal, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 172: 
 173:             // FIX-11K Phase A: observe only. This runs beside the existing exit path and cannot
 174:             // mutate quantity, cash, wallet_position_test, SELL authority, or Production state.
 175:             // It records enough replay-native evidence to evaluate the 2/3/4 streak and
 176:             // peak/giveback experiment matrix after the run instead of pre-selecting thresholds.
 177:             if (open != null && "1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 178:                 defensiveRiskReductionObserver.observe(
 179:                         runId, open.positionId(), signal, latest5m, latest1h,
 180:                         consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells, open.entryPrice(), open.highest());
 181:             }
 182: 
 183:             if (open != null && !exactPriceReplay) {
 184:                 // Pre-V64 fallback only. Exact-parity runs receive mechanical protection from
 185:                 // the persisted Production live-price stream above.
 186:                 ExitDecision exit = evaluateExit(runId, open, signal, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 187:                 if (exit.exit()) {
 188:                     BigDecimal proceeds = signal.getLatestPrice().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
 189:                     BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
 190:                     BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 191:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
 192:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
 193:                     cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
 194:                     realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
 195:                     trades++;
 196:                     if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
 197:                     persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
 198:                     closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
 199:                     // FIX-020 replay parity: a terminal replay exit consumes the same
 200:                     // opportunity evidence boundary as production before any new BUY can form.
 201:                     replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
 202:                     executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());
 203:                                         open = null;
 204:                     // Production PaperTradingService returns immediately after a signal-driven
 205:                     // terminal close; it cannot reopen from that same signal invocation.
 206:                     continue;
 207:                 } else {
 208:                     if (exit.newTakeProfit() != null) {
 209:                         open = rebaseForExtendedTarget(open, exit.newTakeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt())
 210:                                 .withTakeProfit(exit.newTakeProfit());
 211:                         persistProfitLockState(open);
 212:                         persistNearTpState(open);
 213:                     }
 214:                     open = updateProfitLock(open, signal);
 215:                     // FIX-11T: legacy signal-price fallback cannot be tick-exact, but it still
 216:                     // executes the SAME Near-TP policy from the authoritative historical signal
 217:                     // price so older Replay windows do not silently omit the Production rule.
 218:                     if (priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, open.entryTime())) {
 219:                         FallbackNearTpEvaluation nearTpFallback = evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(
 220:                                 runId, open, signal, latest1m, latest5m);
 221:                         open = nearTpFallback.position();
 222:                         if (nearTpFallback.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
 223:                             cash = cash.add(nearTpFallback.partialProceeds(), MC);
 224:                             realized = realized.add(nearTpFallback.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
 225:                         }
 226:                     }
 227:                     persistManagement(runId, signal, open.profitLockActive() ? "PROFIT_LOCK_ACTIVE" : "POSITION_HOLD",
 228:                             open.takeProfit(), open.takeProfit(), open, exit.explanation());
 229:                 }
 230:             }
 231: 
 232:             if (open != null && exactPriceReplay && "1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && bearish(signal.getDecision())) {
 233:                 // Production's candle-close PaperTradingService has one additional SELL authority
 234:                 // after live-price protection: validate the newly generated 1m bearish signal.
 235:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 236:                 if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 237:                     ExitDecision exit = new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 238:                     BigDecimal proceeds = signal.getLatestPrice().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
 239:                     BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
 240:                     BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 241:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
 242:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
 243:                     cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
 244:                     realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
 245:                     trades++;
 246:                     if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
 247:                     persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
 248:                     closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
 249:                     replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
 250:                     executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());
 251:                                         open = null;
 252:                     // Same production invocation cannot both SELL and immediately BUY again.
 253:                     continue;
 254:                 }
 255:             }
 256: 
 257:             // FIX-014 replay parity: production may use a fresh 5m BUY transition only to
 258:             // wake an already-live deferred 1m opportunity. The actual execution/risk plan
 259:             // remains the latest fresh 1m signal returned by the shared production service.
 260:             // All other non-1m signals remain context-only exactly as before.
 261:             TradeSignal executionSignal = signal;
 262:             ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision decision;
 263:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 264:                 int currentAllocation = open == null ? 0 : open.positionPercent();
 265:                 String currentStage = replayStage(currentAllocation);
 266:                 // FIX-026 parity: Recovery/Transition Entry is intentionally NOT reimplemented here.
 267:                 // Replay enters the exact production ExecutionIntelligenceService, which delegates to
 268:                 // RecoveryTransitionService using only candles closed as-of this historical signal.
 269:                 // This protects the ENA 12:55-13:04 KSA regression from replay-only future leakage.
 270:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(signal, currentAllocation, currentStage);
 271:             } else if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && open == null) {
 272:                 ExecutionIntelligenceService.SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup =
 273:                         executionIntelligenceService.evaluateSetupTimeframeWakeup(signal, 0);
 274:                 if (!wakeup.present()) {
 275:                     // FIX-023 replay parity: use the exact same production confirmation wake-up
 276:                     // after liquidity/HTF improves; 5m remains context-only execution authority.
 277:                     wakeup = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeup(signal, 0);
 278:                 }
 279:                 if (!wakeup.present()) continue;
 280:                 executionSignal = wakeup.executionSignal();
 281:                 decision = wakeup.decision();
 282:             } else {
 283:                 continue;
 284:             }
 285: 
 286:             // Warm-up evaluates the SAME production decision service so evidence/opportunity
 287:             // memory is realistic at executionStart, but it never opens or adds to a wallet
 288:             // position before the requested test window. The trigger timestamp (signal) controls
 289:             // the replay window; executionSignal owns price/risk just like production.
 290:             boolean inExecutionWindow = !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
 291:                     && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd);
 292:             LocalDate signalTradeDate = signal.getGeneratedAt().atZone(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate();
 293:             if (!signalTradeDate.equals(replayTradeDate)) {
 294:                 replayTradeDate = signalTradeDate;
 295:                 replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
 296:                 replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
 297:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
 298:                         replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
 299:             }
 300:             // IMPORTANT: Proven/Regression never re-implements pressure readiness, normal BUY
 301:             // routing, or FIX-014 wake-up rules. All are executed by the production
 302:             // ExecutionIntelligenceService above; only wallet/opportunity persistence is shadowed.
 303:             if (!inExecutionWindow) continue;
 304: 
 305:             if (open == null && decision.allowed()) {
 306:                 // FIX-056 Replay parity: resolve the same canonical execution-price authority
 307:                 // after the same Production decision gates, then revalidate Entry Quality at
 308:                 // that price before sizing. A replay without a fresh price event cannot claim
 309:                 // exact execution parity and therefore does not open the position here.
 310:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 311:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 312:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
 313:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(executionSignal, decision, fresh.price());
 314:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
 315:                 int atrPercent = executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() <= 0 ? 100 : executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent();
 316:                 int effectivePercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100,
 317:                         (int)Math.round(atrPercent * decision.positionPercent() / 100.0)));
 318:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 319:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
 320:                         effectivePercent, 0, true, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),

 736:     /**
 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }
 777: 
 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }
 786: 
 787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
 788:         return switch (interval) {
 789:             case "1h" -> 0;
 790:             case "5m" -> 1;
 791:             default -> 2;
 792:         };
 793:     }

  61:     public ReplayStats replay(long runId, String symbol, Instant executionStart, Instant executionEnd,
  62:                               List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals,
  63:                               List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> productionPriceEvents,
  64:                               BooleanSupplier stopRequested) {
  65:         List<TradeSignal> timeline = generatedSignals.stream()
  66:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  67:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt)
  68:                         .thenComparingInt(s -> intervalOrder(s.getInterval())))
  69:                 .toList();
  70: 
  71:         TradeSignal latest1m = null;
  72:         TradeSignal latest5m = null;
  73:         TradeSignal latest1h = null;
  74:         ShadowPosition open = null;
  75:         BigDecimal cash = INITIAL_CAPITAL;
  76:         BigDecimal realized = BigDecimal.ZERO;
  77:         int trades = 0, wins = 0, losses = 0;
  78:         int consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = 0;
  79:         WalletSettings replayWalletSettings = walletSettings();
  80:         BigDecimal replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
  81:                 cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
  82:                 replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
  83:         LocalDate replayTradeDate = null;
  84:         int replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
  85:         // FIX-052: when Production live-price events exist, Replay uses them as the
  86:         // authoritative mechanical-protection clock. Older windows created before V64
  87:         // transparently retain candle/signal-close fallback and are therefore not claimed
  88:         // as tick-exact parity runs.
  89:         List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> livePrices = productionPriceEvents == null
  90:                 ? List.of()
  91:                 : productionPriceEvents.stream()
  92:                     .filter(e -> e != null && e.observedAt() != null && e.price() != null)
  93:                     .sorted(Comparator.comparing(MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent::observedAt))
  94:                     .toList();
  95:         boolean exactPriceReplay = !livePrices.isEmpty();
  96:         int livePriceIndex = 0;
  97:         // FIX-109: normal Regression/Replay runs are Production-Parity by default.
  98:         // Experimental rules require an explicitly opened EXPERIMENTAL scope.
  99:         String replayLogicMode = ExecutionReplayScope.ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY.name();
 100:         defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunStart(runId, symbol);
 101:         oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.startRun(runId, symbol);
 102: 
 103:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, timeline,
 104:                 opportunity -> {
 105:                     TradeSignal latest = opportunity == null ? null : opportunity.getLatestSignal();
 106:                     if (latest != null && latest.getGeneratedAt() != null
 107:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
 108:                             && !latest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
 109:                         persistProductionOpportunity(runId, opportunity);
 110:                     }
 111:                 })) {
 112:         // FIX-122: independent OLD comparison revision; ordinary Replay defaults to NEW.
 113:         java.util.List<Boolean> revisions = jdbcTemplate.query(
 114:                 "SELECT enabled FROM fix122_replay_revision WHERE test_run_id=?",
 115:                 (rs,row) -> rs.getBoolean(1), runId);
 116:         replayScope.fix122Enabled(revisions == null || revisions.isEmpty() || revisions.get(0));
 117:         for (TradeSignal signal : timeline) {
 118:             // FIX-090: shadow execution is frequently cancellable without affecting Production.
 119:             if (stopRequested != null && stopRequested.getAsBoolean()) {
 120:                 throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
 121:             }
 122:             // FIX-052 exact ordering: BinanceKlineService invokes live position protection
 123:             // from the 1m price update BEFORE publishing the candle-close analysis event.
 124:             // Consume all persisted Production prices up to this signal timestamp before
 125:             // making the freshly generated signal visible to position management.
 126:             while (livePriceIndex < livePrices.size()
 127:                     && (stopRequested == null || !stopRequested.getAsBoolean())
 128:                     && !livePrices.get(livePriceIndex).observedAt().isAfter(signal.getGeneratedAt())) {
 129:                 MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent live = livePrices.get(livePriceIndex++);
 130:                 replayScope.referencePrice(symbol, live.observedAt(), live.price());
 131:                 // FIX-11Q: advance only the isolated counterfactual grace position. This never mutates
 132:                 // the baseline Shadow position/cash/trades and therefore cannot change Replay parity.
 133:                 oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 134:                 if (open != null && !live.observedAt().isBefore(executionStart) && !live.observedAt().isAfter(executionEnd)) {
 135:                     LivePriceEvaluation protection = evaluateLivePrice(runId, symbol, open, live, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 136:                     open = protection.position();
 137:                     if (protection.partialProceeds().signum() > 0) {
 138:                         cash = cash.add(protection.partialProceeds(), MC);
 139:                         realized = realized.add(protection.partialRealizedPnl(), MC);
 140:                     }
 141:                     ExitDecision liveExit = protection.decision();
 142:                     if (liveExit.exit()) {
 143:                         BigDecimal proceeds = live.price().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
 144:                         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
 145:                         BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), live.price());
 146:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
 147:                         BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
 148:                         cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
 149:                         realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
 150:                         trades++;
 151:                         if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
 152:                         // wallet_execution_test stores this terminal leg; wallet_position_test stores
 153:                         // the whole lifecycle including any earlier FIX-11T partial realization.
 154:                         persistSellAtPrice(runId, symbol, live, latest1m, open, liveExit, pnl, pnlPct);
 155:                         closePositionAtPrice(runId, open.positionId(), live, liveExit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
 156:                         replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), live.observedAt(), liveExit.reason());
 157:                         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, latest1m, liveExit.reason());
 158:                                                 open = null;
 159:                     }
 160:                 }
 161:             }
 162: 
 163:             replayScope.reference(signal.getGeneratedAt());
 164:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 165:                 latest1m = signal;
 166:                 consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells = signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
 167:                         ? consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells + 1 : 0;
 168:             }
 169:             if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest5m = signal;
 170:             if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval())) latest1h = signal;
 171:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.onSignal(runId, signal, latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 172: 
 173:             // FIX-11K Phase A: observe only. This runs beside the existing exit path and cannot
 174:             // mutate quantity, cash, wallet_position_test, SELL authority, or Production state.
 175:             // It records enough replay-native evidence to evaluate the 2/3/4 streak and
 176:             // peak/giveback experiment matrix after the run instead of pre-selecting thresholds.
 177:             if (open != null && "1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 178:                 defensiveRiskReductionObserver.observe(
 179:                         runId, open.positionId(), signal, latest5m, latest1h,
 180:                         consecutiveFinalOneMinuteStrongSells, open.entryPrice(), open.highest());
 181:             }
 182: 
 183:             if (open != null && !exactPriceReplay) {
 184:                 // Pre-V64 fallback only. Exact-parity runs receive mechanical protection from
 185:                 // the persisted Production live-price stream above.
 392:     /**
 393:      * FIX-052: mechanical position protection evaluated from the exact Production
 394:      * 1m live-price observation stream. The order mirrors LivePositionProtectionService:
 395:      * TAKE_PROFIT -> STOP_LOSS -> PROFIT_LOCK -> normal HTF exit. The newly closed
 396:      * 1m signal is intentionally not visible yet; BinanceKlineService calls onPrice()
 397:      * before publishing the candle-close event in Production.
 398:      */
 399:     private LivePriceEvaluation evaluateLivePrice(long runId, String symbol, ShadowPosition p,
 400:                                                    MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
 401:                                                    TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 402:         BigDecimal price = event.price();
 403:         ShadowPosition current = p;
 404: 
 405:         if (current.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(current.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 406:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(

 219:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(one, five, hour);
 220:             if (lockDecision.exit()) {
 221:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " " + lockDecision.explanation();
 222:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, lockDecision.code(), reason)) {
 223:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, lockDecision.code(), reason, one);
 224:                     log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK exit: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 225:                             symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.code());
 226:                 }
 227:                 return;
 228:             }
 229:             log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK protected hold: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 230:                     symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.explanation());
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         // Normal signal exits remain active even when Profit Lock is disabled. Higher-timeframe
 234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
 235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
 236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 237:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation())) {
 238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);
 239:                 log.info("Live signal-driven exit: symbol={}, price={}, reason={}", symbol, price, normalExit.code());
 240:             }
 241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
 242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
 243:             return;
 244:         }
 245: 
 246:         // FIX-11T: Near-TP Failure Protection is deliberately LAST. TP/SL/profit-lock and
 247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
 248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
 249:     }
 250: 
 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.
 256:         Instant evaluatedAt = Instant.now();
 257:         oneMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "1m", oneMinute, evaluatedAt);
 258:         fiveMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "5m", fiveMinute, evaluatedAt);
 259: 
 260:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 261:                 managed.getNearTpState() == null ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState(),
 262:                 managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), managed.getNearTpBearishStreak(),
 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());
 264: 
 265:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 266:                 state, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(),
 267:                 price, evaluatedAt, oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 268: 
 269:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State next = evaluation.state();
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()
  90:         if (signal == null || signal.getId() == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Persisted signal required");
  91:         return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(), false, this::processSignalBody);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
  95:     public Optional<PaperPosition> recoverRegisteredSignal(long signalId) {
  96:         return processingCoordinator.process(signalId, true, this::processSignalBody);
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     /** FIX-132: explicit delivery ownership, never a natural-key inference. */
 100:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
 101:     public Optional<PaperPosition> processSharedSignal(TradeSignal signal, long sourceEventId) {
 102:         return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(),false,this::processSignalBody,sourceEventId);
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     /** FIX-127: existing decision order; invoked inside the coordinator's transaction. */
 106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
 107:         if (signal == null) {
 108:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Trade signal is required");
 109:         }
 110: 
 111:         String symbol = normalizeSymbol(signal.getSymbol());
 112: 
 113:         // FIX-127: position management persists advice and can request POSITION_STOP_LOSS.
 114:         // Keep its existing decision order, but acquire the initial managed lock before entering here.
 115:         if (positionManagementService != null) {
 116:             try {
 117:                 positionManagementService.analyze(signal);
 118:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 119:                 log.warn("Position advisory failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage());
 120:             }
 121:         }
 122:         Optional<PaperPosition> openPosition = positionRepository
 123:                 .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN);
 124: 
 125:         if (openPosition.isPresent()) {
 126:             PaperPosition position = openPosition.get();
 127:             BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
 128: 
 129:             // Never treat a delayed timeframe candle close as the current mark price.
 130:             // Context from that signal is still processed below, but TP/SL/profit-lock
 131:             // mechanics require a post-entry market observation. Live production protection
 132:             // is independently driven by LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice(...).
 133:             boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
 134:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock = authoritativePrice
 135:                     ? dynamicProfitLockService.evaluate(signal)
 136:                     : DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation.inactive(
 137:                             "Signal price predates the open position and is context-only for mechanical protection.");
 138: 
 139:             // FIX-118 Production trace for the independent signal-price Profit Lock authority.
 140:             // The explicit ProfitLockState now also shows whether the historical lock is ACTIVE
 141:             // or temporarily non-executable while TP extension is rebasing.
 142:             if (authoritativePrice && (profitLock.active() || profitLock.triggered() || profitLock.rebasing())) {
 143:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_PATH] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, price={}, " +
 144:                                 "active={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 145:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, signal.getInterval(), price,
 146:                         profitLock.active(), profitLock.progressPercent(), profitLock.activationPercent(),
 147:                         profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.state(), profitLock.triggered());
 148:             }
 149: 
 150:             // FIX-067: The wallet-managed position is the authoritative Production source for
 151:             // a dynamically extended take-profit.  paper_position historically retained the
 152:             // entry-time TP, so a fresh 1m signal could close against that stale threshold even
 153:             // after LivePositionProtectionService had persisted TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED.
 154:             // Prefer the OPEN managed TP and repair the paper snapshot when they differ. This is
 155:             // deliberately NOT a TP-policy change: it only makes both existing exit paths compare
 156:             // against the same already-approved target.
 157:             BigDecimal authoritativeTakeProfit = position.getTakeProfit();
 158:             WalletManagedPosition managedPosition = walletManagedPositionRepository
 159:                     .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 160:                     .orElse(null);
 161:             if (managedPosition != null && managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null) {
 162:                 authoritativeTakeProfit = managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 163:                 if (position.getTakeProfit() == null
 164:                         || position.getTakeProfit().compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) != 0) {
 165:                     position.setTakeProfit(authoritativeTakeProfit);
 166:                     positionRepository.save(position);
 167:                 }
 168:             }
 169: 
 170:             if (authoritativePrice && authoritativeTakeProfit != null
 171:                     && price.compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) >= 0) {
 172:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 173:                         "TAKE_PROFIT", "Price reached the authoritative configured take-profit target."));
 174:             }
 175: 
 176:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.triggered()) {
 177:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}",
 178:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.progressPercent());
 179:                 return Optional.of(closeFromProfitLock(position, signal, profitLock));
 180:             }
 181: 
 182:             if (authoritativePrice && price.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
 183:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.STOPPED,
 184:                         "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the configured stop loss."));
 185:             }
 186: 
 187:             // FIX-118: in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the old Profit Lock cannot execute, but the
 188:             // direct protected-profit floor still applies. Stop Loss remains absolute and
 189:             // therefore keeps priority immediately above this branch.
 190:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 191:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}",
 192:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.hardProfitFloor());
 193:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 194:                         "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", profitLock.explanation()));
 195:             }
 196: 
 197:             if (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL
 198:                     || signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 199:                 if (profitLock.active()) {
 200:                     log.info("Normal wallet SELL suppressed because Dynamic Profit Lock is active: signalId={}, symbol={}, protectedPrice={}, currentPrice={}",
 201:                             signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), profitLock.lockPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 202:                     return Optional.of(position);
 203:                 }
 204:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation =
 205:                         executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 206:                 if (validation.allowed()) {
 207:                     return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 208:                             signal.getDecision().name(), signal.getExplanation()));
 209:                 }
 210:                 log.info("Normal wallet SELL rejected: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, reason={}, detail={}",
 211:                         signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(),
 212:                         validation.code(), validation.explanation());
 213:             }
 214: 
 215:             // Progressive Position Building may add to an existing position only after
 216:             // TP / Profit Lock / SL / validated SELL logic had first priority.
 217:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && !profitLock.active()) {
 218:                 WalletManagedPosition managed = walletManagedPositionRepository
 219:                         .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 220:                         .orElse(null);
 221:                 if (managed != null) {
 222:                     ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision addDecision =
 223:                             executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(
 224:                                     signal,
 225:                                     managed.getAllocatedPositionPercent(),
 226:                                     managed.getEntryStage());
 227:                     if (addDecision.allowed()) {
 228:                         if (walletTradeRepository
 229:                                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
 230:                                         signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 231:                                 .isPresent()) {
 232:                             return Optional.of(position);
 233:                         }
 234:                         int addPercent = addDecision.positionPercent();
 235:                         // FIX-056: progressive adds use the same fresh canonical price authority
 236:                         // as initial entries. Signal.latestPrice remains decision-time evidence only.
 237:                         ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 238:                                 .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
 239:                         if (fresh == null) {
 240:                             log.info("Progressive BUY skipped: no fresh execution price for {}", symbol);
 241:                             return Optional.of(position);
 242:                         }
 243:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision freshDecision =
 244:                                 executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, addDecision, fresh.price());
 245:                         if (!freshDecision.allowed()) return Optional.of(position);
 246:                         addPercent = freshDecision.positionPercent();
 247:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
 248:                                 executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, fresh.price());
 249:                         boolean walletAdded = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(
 250:                                 signal, fresh.price(), fresh.observedAt(), addPercent,
  50:         String symbol = signal.getSymbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  51:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
  52:                 .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  53:                 .orElse(null);
  54:         if (!validPosition(position)) return Optional.empty();
  55: 
  56:         if (analysisRepository.existsByWalletPositionIdAndTradeSignalId(position.getId(), signal.getId())) {
  57:             return analysisRepository.findTopByWalletPositionIdOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc(position.getId());
  58:         }
  59: 
  60:         BigDecimal entry = nvl(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
  61:         BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
  62:         BigDecimal pnl = price.subtract(entry).multiply(position.getQuantity());
  63:         BigDecimal pnlPercent = percentageChange(entry, price);
  64:         long holdingMinutes = Math.max(0,
  65:                 Duration.between(position.getOpenedAt(), Instant.now()).toMinutes());
  66: 
  67:         // A TradeSignal price can belong to a candle that closed BEFORE this position opened.
  68:         // ALLOUSDT incident 2026-08-17: a delayed 5m signal carried 0.2806 from the
  69:         // 18:15-18:19 candle and falsely stopped a position opened at 18:23 @ 0.2822.
  70:         // Such signals are still valid thesis/MTF context, but they are forbidden from
  71:         // driving price-based hard protection. Live TP/SL is handled by
  72:         // LivePositionProtectionService from the actual live Binance price stream.
  73:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
  74:         PositionRecommendation hardRecommendation = authoritativePrice
  75:                 ? hardRiskRecommendation(position, price)
  76:                 : null;
  77: 
  78:         PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
  79:                 position.getEntryTrendScore(), position.getEntryStructureScore(),
  80:                 position.getEntryMomentumScore(), position.getEntryVolumeScore(),
  81:                 position.getEntryConfidence(), position.getEntryTotalScore(), signal);
  82:         int trendDeterioration = thesis.trendPressure();
  83:         int momentumExhaustion = thesis.momentumPressure();
  84:         int profitProtection = profitProtection(pnlPercent, thesis);
  85:         int riskEvents = authoritativePrice ? stopRiskPressure(position, price) : 0;
  86:         int opportunityCost = 0; // intentionally excluded from v1
  87:         int exitScore = Math.min(25, trendDeterioration + momentumExhaustion
  88:                 + profitProtection + riskEvents);
  89: 
  90:         int authority = timeframeAuthority(signal.getInterval());
  91:         PositionRecommendation recommendation = hardRecommendation != null
  92:                 ? hardRecommendation
  93:                 : recommendation(exitScore, profitProtection, pnlPercent, authority);
  94:         int confidence = confidence(exitScore, recommendation, hardRecommendation != null);
  95: 
  96:         List<String> evidence = evidence(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
  97:                 trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
  98:                 riskEvents, authority, hardRecommendation);
  99:         String explanation = recommendation + " advisory: " + String.join("; ", evidence);
 100: 
 101:         Map<String, Object> details = details(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
 102:                 trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
 103:                 riskEvents, opportunityCost, exitScore, authority, evidence);
 104: 
 105:         PositionAnalysis saved = analysisRepository.save(PositionAnalysis.builder()
 106:                 .walletPosition(position)
 107:                 .tradeSignal(signal)
 108:                 .symbol(symbol)
 109:                 .intervalCode(signal.getInterval())
 110:                 .entryPriceUsdt(entry)
 111:                 .currentPriceUsdt(price)
 112:                 .unrealizedPnlUsdt(pnl)
 113:                 .unrealizedPnlPercent(pnlPercent)
 114:                 .holdingMinutes(holdingMinutes)
 115:                 .trendDeteriorationScore(trendDeterioration)
 116:                 .momentumExhaustionScore(momentumExhaustion)
 117:                 .profitProtectionScore(profitProtection)
 118:                 .riskEventScore(riskEvents)
 119:                 .opportunityCostScore(opportunityCost)
 120:                 .exitScore(exitScore)
 121:                 .recommendation(recommendation)
 122:                 .confidence(confidence)
 123:                 .explanation(limit(explanation, 2000))
 124:                 .detailsJson(toJson(details))
 125:                 .advisoryOnly(true)
 126:                 .analyzedAt(Instant.now())
 127:                 .build());
 128: 
 129:         if (recommendation == PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS) {
 130:             boolean applied = walletAutoExecutionService.executePositionStopLoss(saved);
 131:             if (applied) {
 132:                 saved.setAdvisoryOnly(false);
 133:                 details.put("advisoryOnly", false);
 134:                 details.put("walletApplied", true);
 135:                 details.put("walletExecutionReason", "POSITION_STOP_LOSS");
 136:                 saved.setDetailsJson(toJson(details));
 137:                 saved = analysisRepository.save(saved);
 138:                 log.warn("Position STOP_LOSS applied to wallet: position={}, analysis={}, signal={}, symbol={}",
 139:                         position.getId(), saved.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol);
 140:             } else {
 141:                 log.error("Position STOP_LOSS was generated but not applied to wallet: position={}, analysis={}, signal={}, symbol={}",
 142:                         position.getId(), saved.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol);
 143:             }
 144:         }
 145: 
 146:         log.info("Position analysis created: position={}, signal={}, symbol={}, recommendation={}, exitScore={}/25, advisoryOnly={}",
 147:                 position.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol, recommendation, exitScore, saved.isAdvisoryOnly());
 148:         return Optional.of(saved);
 149:     }
 150: 
 151:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 152:     public List<PositionAnalysisView> latest() {
 153:         return analysisRepository.findTop100ByOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc().stream()
 154:                 .map(this::view)
 155:                 .toList();
 156:     }
 157: 
 158:     private boolean validSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 159:         return signal != null && signal.getId() != null && signal.getSymbol() != null
 160:                 && signal.getLatestPrice() != null && signal.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0;
 161:     }
 162: 
 163:     private boolean validPosition(WalletManagedPosition position) {
 164:         return position != null && position.getId() != null && position.getQuantity() != null
 165:                 && position.getQuantity().signum() > 0;
 166:     }
 167: 
 168:     private PositionRecommendation hardRiskRecommendation(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 169:         if (p.getStopLossUsdt() != null && p.getStopLossUsdt().signum() > 0
 170:                 && price.compareTo(p.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 171:             return PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS;
 172:         }
 173:         if (p.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && p.getTakeProfitUsdt().signum() > 0
 174:                 && price.compareTo(p.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
 175:             return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
 176:         }
 177:         return null;
 178:     }
 179: 
 180:     private int profitProtection(BigDecimal pnlPercent, PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis) {
 181:         if (pnlPercent.signum() <= 0) return 0;
 182:         int score = 0;
 183:         if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(5)) >= 0) score += 2;
 184:         else if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(2)) >= 0) score += 1;
 185:         if (thesis.trendDrop() >= 6 || thesis.structureDrop() >= 3) score += 2;
 186:         if (thesis.momentumDrop() >= 5 || thesis.totalDrop() >= 15) score += 1;
 187:         return Math.min(5, score);
 188:     }
 189: 
 190:     private int stopRiskPressure(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Shared production/replay exit authority for an already-open long position.
   9:  *
  10:  * The policy deliberately separates short-timeframe noise from a genuine
  11:  * higher-timeframe breakdown:
  12:  *  - a 1m pullback alone never closes the position;
  13:  *  - the existing 1m SELL + bearish 5m confirmation remains valid;
  14:  *  - a fresh 1h SELL may close a position when 5m is no longer supportive;
  15:  *  - a Profit Lock breach is tolerated while 5m/1h still support the bullish thesis.
  16:  */
  17: @Component
  18: public class PositionExitPolicy {
  19: 
  20:     public Evaluation evaluateNormalExit(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  21:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  22:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  23:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  24: 
  25:         boolean oneHourBreakdown = bearish(oneDecision)
  26:                 && fiveMinute != null
  27:                 && !supportive(fiveDecision);
  28:         if (oneHourBreakdown) {
  29:             return Evaluation.exit("HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED",
  30:                     "1h turned " + label(oneDecision)
  31:                             + " while 5m is no longer supportive (" + label(fiveDecision)
  32:                             + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
  33:         }
  34: 
  35:         boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
  36:                 && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
  37:                 && bearish(currentDecision)
  38:                 && fiveMinute != null
  39:                 && bearish(fiveDecision)
  40:                 && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
  41:         if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
  42:             return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
  43:                     "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
  47:                 + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  48:                 + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  49:                 + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  50:     }
  51: 
  52:     public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  53:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  54:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  55:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  56: 
  57:         // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
  58:         if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
  59:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  60:                     "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
  61:                             + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  62:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  63:                             + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
  64:         }
  65: 
  66:         // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
  67:         if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
  68:                 && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
  69:                 && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
  70:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  71:                     "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
  72:                             + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  73:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  74:                             + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
  75:         }
  76: 
  77:         if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
  78:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
  79:                     "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
  80:                             + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
  84:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
  85:                     "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
  86:         }
  87: 
  88:         return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
  89:                 "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
  90:                         + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  91:                         + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  92:                         + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
  96:         return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
 100:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY
 101:                 || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY
 102:                 || decision == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 106:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 107:     }
 108: 
 109:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 110:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 111:     }
 112: 
 113:     private String label(SignalDecision decision) {
 114:         return decision == null ? "MISSING" : decision.name();
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     public record Evaluation(boolean exit, String code, String explanation) {
 118:         public static Evaluation exit(String code, String explanation) {
 119:             return new Evaluation(true, code, explanation);
 120:         }
 121: 
 122:         public static Evaluation hold(String explanation) {
 123:             return new Evaluation(false, "HOLD", explanation);
 124:         }
 125: 
 126:         public static Evaluation hold(String code, String explanation) {
 127:             return new Evaluation(false, code, explanation);
 128:         }
 129:     }

  36: public class DynamicProfitLockService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  39:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  40: 
  41:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positionRepository;
