  45:     @Transactional
  46:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal signal) {
  47:         if (signal == null) {
  48:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  49:         }
  50:         return evaluatePrice(signal.getSymbol(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     /**
  54:      * Evaluates Dynamic Profit Lock directly from a live market price.
  55:      * This path is used by live position protection between candle-close signals.
  56:      *
  57:      * FIX-118 behavioral phase:
  58:      * TP_EXTENSION_REBASE preserves the historically earned lock in persistence but makes
  59:      * it non-executable until progress against the CURRENT TP reaches the configured
  60:      * activation threshold again. This gives an approved continuation meaningful room
  61:      * without forgetting that profit protection had already been earned.
  62:      */
  63:     @Transactional
  64:     public Evaluation evaluatePrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal currentPrice) {
  65:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || currentPrice == null
  66:                 || currentPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  67:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  68:         }
  69: 
  70:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  71:         WalletManagedPosition position = positionRepository
  72:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  73:                 .orElse(null);
  74:         if (position == null) {
  75:             return Evaluation.inactive("No open wallet position exists.");
  76:         }
  77: 
  78:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  79:         if (!settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled()) {
  80:             return Evaluation.inactive("Dynamic Profit Lock is disabled in Administration.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         BigDecimal entry = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
  84:         BigDecimal target = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
  85:         BigDecimal current = currentPrice;
  86:         if (entry == null || target == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  87:             return Evaluation.inactive("The position does not have a valid long take-profit distance.");
  88:         }
  89: 
  90:         ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings);
  91:         BigDecimal activation = profile.activationPercent();
  92:         BigDecimal initialLock = profile.initialLockPercent();
  93:         BigDecimal trailStep = profile.trailStepPercent();
  94:         BigDecimal previousHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
  95:         boolean previousPersistedActive = position.isProfitLockActive();
  96:         BigDecimal previousLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
  97:         BigDecimal previousProgress = position.getProfitLockProgressPercent();
  98:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
  99: 
 100:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest == null ? current : previousHighest.max(current);
 101:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(entry, target, highest);
 102:         BigDecimal lockPrice = previousLock;
 103:         ProfitLockState state = previousState;
 104:         boolean executableActive;
 105:         boolean triggered;
 106: 
 107:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 108:             // Keep the legacy active flag/lock persisted so history is not lost, but do not
 109:             // allow the superseded geometry to trigger an exit during the rebase window.
 110:             executableActive = false;
 111:             triggered = false;
 112:         } else {
 113:             // FIX-118 refinement: when a TP-extension rebase earns activation against the
 114:             // CURRENT target, rebuild the executable lock from the current geometry instead
 115:             // of carrying the historical pre-extension lock through ProfitLockPolicy's
 116:             // monotonic previous-lock rule.
 117:             boolean freshGeometryActivation = previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 118:             ProfitLockPolicy.State policyState = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 119:                     entry, target, current, highest,
 120:                     freshGeometryActivation ? false : (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || previousPersistedActive),
 121:                     freshGeometryActivation ? null : previousLock,
 122:                     true, activation, initialLock, trailStep);
 123:             highest = policyState.highestPrice();
 124:             progress = policyState.progressPercent();
 125:             lockPrice = policyState.lockPrice();
 126:             executableActive = policyState.active();
 127:             triggered = policyState.triggered();
 128:             state = executableActive ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 129:         }
 130: 
 131:         // During REBASE the legacy flag intentionally remains true: protection was historically
 132:         // earned. The explicit state is now the sole execution authority.
 133:         boolean persistedActive = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 134:                 ? true
 135:                 : executableActive;
 136: 
 137:         Instant activatedAt = position.getProfitLockActivatedAt();
 138:         if (persistedActive && activatedAt == null) activatedAt = Instant.now();
 139:         Instant rebaseStartedAt = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 140:                 ? position.getProfitLockRebaseStartedAt()
 141:                 : null;
 142: 
 143:         boolean changed = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null

 145:                 || position.isProfitLockActive() != persistedActive
 146:                 || different(position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(), lockPrice)
 147:                 || different(position.getProfitLockProgressPercent(), progress)
 148:                 || position.getProfitLockState() != state
 149:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(position.getProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(), rebaseStartedAt)
 150:                 || (position.getProfitLockActivatedAt() == null && activatedAt != null);
 151: 
 152:         if (changed) {
 153:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(highest);
 154:             position.setProfitLockActive(persistedActive);
 155:             position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(lockPrice);
 156:             position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 157:             position.setProfitLockActivatedAt(activatedAt);
 158:             position.setProfitLockState(state);
 159:             position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(rebaseStartedAt);
 160:             position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());

 162:         }

 167: 
 168:         if (changed || triggered || hardFloorTriggered || previousState != state) {

 106:         }
 107: 
 108:         return Optional.of(
 109:                 saveTechnicalIndicator(snapshot)
 110:         );
 111:     }
 112: 
 113:     /**
 114:      * Regression-only calculation path. Computes the exact technical snapshot as of
 115:      * the supplied historical candle without persisting a technical_indicator row.
 116:      */
 117:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 118:     public Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 119:             String symbol,
 120:             String intervalCode,
 121:             Instant candleOpenTime
 122:     ) {
 123:         if (candleOpenTime == null) {
 124:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression candle open time is required");
 125:         }
 126:         return calculateSnapshotFromDatabase(symbol, intervalCode, candleOpenTime);
 127:     }
 128: 
 129:     /**
 130:      * FIX-11H REPLAY/PARITY INFRASTRUCTURE ONLY. Production calculateAndPersist() never calls
 131:      * this overload. The only difference is where the already-closed candle list comes from;
 132:      * calculateSnapshot() below remains the exact shared indicator math.
 133:      */
 134:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 135:     public Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 136:             String symbol, String intervalCode, Instant candleOpenTime, ReplayDataset dataset) {
 137:         if (candleOpenTime == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression candle open time is required");
 138:         String normalisedSymbol = normaliseSymbol(symbol);
 139:         String normalisedInterval = normaliseInterval(intervalCode);
 140:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(
 141:                 dataset.closedCandlesAtOrBefore(normalisedSymbol, normalisedInterval, candleOpenTime, HISTORY_LIMIT));
 142:         if (candles.size() < MINIMUM_CANDLES) return Optional.empty();
 143:         Collections.reverse(candles);
 144:         return Optional.of(calculateSnapshot(normalisedSymbol, normalisedInterval, candles));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     /** ReplayDataset loader uses the same history limit as the indicator service; no duplicated magic number. */
 148:     public int regressionHistoryLimit() { return HISTORY_LIMIT; }
 149: 
 150:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 151:     public Optional<TechnicalIndicator> getLatest(
 152:             String symbol,
 153:             String intervalCode
 154:     ) {
 155:         return indicatorRepository
 156:                 .findTopBySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc(
 157:                         normaliseSymbol(symbol),
 158:                         normaliseInterval(intervalCode)
 159:                 );
 160:     }
 161: 
 162:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 163:     public List<TechnicalIndicator> getHistory(
 164:             String symbol,
 165:             String intervalCode
 166:     ) {
 167:         return indicatorRepository
 168:                 .findTop100BySymbolAndIntervalCodeOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc(
 169:                         normaliseSymbol(symbol),
 170:                         normaliseInterval(intervalCode)
 171:                 );
 172:     }
 173: 
 174:     private Optional<IndicatorSnapshot>
 175:     calculateSnapshotFromDatabase(
 176:             String symbol,
 177:             String intervalCode,
 178:             Instant maxOpenTime
 179:     ) {
 180:         String normalisedSymbol =
 181:                 normaliseSymbol(symbol);
 182: 
 183:         String normalisedInterval =
 184:                 normaliseInterval(intervalCode);
 185: 
 186:         List<Candle> candles =
 187:                 new ArrayList<>(
 188:                         maxOpenTime == null
 189:                                 ? candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
 190:                                         normalisedSymbol,
 191:                                         normalisedInterval,
 192:                                         PageRequest.of(0, HISTORY_LIMIT)
 193:                                 )
 194:                                 : candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 195:                                         normalisedSymbol,
 196:                                         normalisedInterval,
 197:                                         maxOpenTime,
 198:                                         PageRequest.of(0, HISTORY_LIMIT)
 199:                                 )
 200:                 );
 201: 
 202:         if (candles.size() < MINIMUM_CANDLES) {
 203:             log.warn(
 204:                     "Indicator calculation skipped: symbol={}, interval={}, closedCandles={}, required={}",
 205:                     normalisedSymbol,
 206:                     normalisedInterval,
 207:                     candles.size(),
 208:                     MINIMUM_CANDLES
 209:             );
 210:             return Optional.empty();
 211:         }
 212: 
 213:         Collections.reverse(candles);
 214: 
 215:         log.info(
 216:         	    "Calculating indicators before snapshot for symbol={}, interval={}",
 217:         	    symbol,
 218:         	    intervalCode
 219:         	);
 220:         
 130:      * It reads the latest already-persisted technical indicator.
 131:      */
 132:     @Transactional
 133:     public TradeSignal analyze(String symbol, String interval) {
 134:         TechnicalIndicator indicator = technicalIndicatorService
 135:                 .getLatest(symbol, interval)
 136:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException(
 137:                         "No technical indicator found for " + symbol + " " + interval
 138:                 ));
 139: 
 140:         return analyze(indicator);
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /**
 144:      * Automatic entry point. It receives the TechnicalIndicator row that was
 145:      * just saved by TechnicalIndicatorService and never recalculates candles.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional
 148:     public TradeSignal analyze(TechnicalIndicator indicator) {
 149:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 150:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, Instant.now(), false, false);
 151:         // FIX-153: physical createdAt and persisted mode distinguish live latency from as-of history.
 152:         signal.setSignalMode("LIVE");
 153:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: 
 198:     /**
 199:      * FIX-11M: Called only by RegressionTestWorker around the fresh-signal stage.
 200:      * This activates timing observation; it does not alter buildSignal inputs, outputs,
 201:      * ordering, thresholds, persistence, or any Production execution path.
 202:      */
 203:     public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 204:         replayAnalysisProfiler.set(new ReplayAnalysisProfiler(runId));
 205:     }
 206: 
 207:     /**
 208:      * FIX-11M: Emit one aggregate Replay-only summary and always clear thread-local state.
 209:      */
 210:     public void finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 211:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 212:         replayAnalysisProfiler.remove();
 213:         if (profiler == null) {
 214:             log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_MISSING run={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false", runId);
 215:             return;
 216:         }
 217:         profiler.logSummary(log, runId);
 218:     }
 219: 
 220:     private TradeSignal buildSignal(IndicatorSnapshot i, Instant signalGeneratedAt, boolean historicalReplay,
 221:                                     boolean regressionReplay) {
 222:         String symbol = i.symbol();
 223:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = regressionReplay ? replayAnalysisProfiler.get() : null;
 224:         long profileStartedNs;
 225: 
 226:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 227:         boolean sentimentEnabled = sentimentService.isEnabled();
 228:         SentimentOverview sentimentOverview = sentimentService.overviewAsOf(symbol, signalGeneratedAt);
 229:         BigDecimal sentiment = sentimentOverview.weightedScore();
 230:         boolean sentimentAvailable = hasUsableSentimentCoverage(sentimentEnabled, sentimentOverview);
 231:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.SENTIMENT, profileStartedNs);
 232: 
 233:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 234:         MarketFundamental fundamental = fundamentalService.latestAsOf(symbol, signalGeneratedAt).orElse(null);
 235:         boolean fundamentalAvailable = fundamentalService.isAvailable(fundamental, signalGeneratedAt);
 236:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.FUNDAMENTALS, profileStartedNs);
 237: 
 238:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 239:         IndicatorSnapshot previous = previousSnapshot(i);
 240:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.PREVIOUS_SNAPSHOT, profileStartedNs);
 241: 
 242:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 243:         TrendStructureResult trendStructure = trendStructureService.evaluate(i);
 244:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.TREND_STRUCTURE, profileStartedNs);
 245: 
 246:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 247:         MovingAverageBreakdown movingAverages = movingAverageScore(i, previous, trendStructure);
 248:         MomentumBreakdown momentumBreakdown = momentumScore(i);
 249:         BandsVolumeBreakdown bandsVolume = bandsVolumeScore(i, previous);
 250:         int trend = movingAverages.total();
 251:         int volume = bandsVolume.total();
 252:         int momentum = momentumBreakdown.total();
 253:         int sentimentPoints = sentimentAvailable ? sentimentScore(sentiment) : 0;
 254:         FundamentalScoreResult fundamentalBreakdown = fundamentalScoringService.score(fundamentalAvailable ? fundamental : null);
 255:         int fundamentals = fundamentalAvailable ? fundamentalBreakdown.total() : 0;
 256:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.BASE_SCORING, profileStartedNs);
 257: 
 258:         int baseRawTotal = trend + volume + momentum + sentimentPoints + fundamentals;
 259:         int baseMaximumAvailableScore = MAX_TREND_SCORE
 260:                 + MAX_VOLUME_SCORE
 261:                 + MAX_MOMENTUM_SCORE
 262:                 + (fundamentalAvailable ? MAX_FUNDAMENTAL_SCORE : 0)
 263:                 + (sentimentAvailable ? MAX_SENTIMENT_SCORE : 0);
 264: 
 265:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 266:         AtrRiskAssessment atrRisk = atrRiskService.assess(i);
 267:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.ATR_RISK, profileStartedNs);
 268: 
 269:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 270:         MarketRegimeAssessment detectedRegimeAssessment = marketRegimeService.assess(i, trendStructure);
 271:         RegimeStateService.Decision regimeState = regimeStateService.apply(
 272:                 symbol, i.intervalCode(), i.candleOpenTime(), detectedRegimeAssessment);
 273:         MarketRegimeAssessment regimeAssessment = regimeState.effectiveAssessment();
 274:         MarketRegime marketRegime = regimeAssessment.regime();
 275:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.REGIME, profileStartedNs);
 276: 
 277:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 278:         MarketContextSnapshot marketContext = marketContextService.build(
 279:                 i, atrRisk, sentimentOverview, sentimentEnabled, signalGeneratedAt, historicalReplay);
 280:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.MARKET_CONTEXT, profileStartedNs);
 281: 
 282:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 283:         StrategyProfile strategyProfile = marketStrategyService.select(regimeAssessment, marketContext);
 284:         atrRisk = atrRiskService.applyStrategyEntryPlan(atrRisk, i, strategyProfile.strategy());
 285:         StrategyScoreResult strategyScore = marketStrategyService.score(
 286:                 strategyProfile,
 287:                 trend,
 288:                 volume,
 289:                 momentum,
 290:                 sentimentPoints,
 291:                 fundamentals,
 292:                 sentimentAvailable,
 293:                 fundamentalAvailable
 294:         );
 295:         strategyScore = marketStrategyService.constrainBreakoutCandidate(regimeAssessment, strategyScore);
 296:         strategyScore = marketStrategyService.promoteEarlyBreakout(
 297:                 strategyProfile, strategyScore, marketContext, atrRisk, regimeAssessment, trendStructure);
 298:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.STRATEGY, profileStartedNs);
 299: 
 300:         int rawTotal = strategyScore.rawScore();
 301:         int maximumAvailableScore = strategyScore.maximumScore();
 302:         int total = strategyScore.normalizedScore();
 303:         SignalDecision baseDecision = strategyScore.decision();
 304:         SignalDecision atrAdjustedDecision = baseDecision;
 305:         // ATR controls entry execution, not bullish direction. Keep BUY as BUY for
 306:         // REDUCED_POSITION, PULLBACK_ENTRY and WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT. Only an
 307:         // extreme NO_ENTRY extension downgrades the directional recommendation.
 308:         if (atrRisk.entryType() == com.crypto.domain.AtrEntryType.NO_ENTRY
 309:                 && (atrAdjustedDecision == SignalDecision.BUY || atrAdjustedDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY)) {
 310:             atrAdjustedDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
 311:         }
 312: 
 313:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 314:         MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult confluence = multiTimeframeConfluenceService.evaluate(
 315:                 symbol, i.intervalCode(), atrAdjustedDecision, signalGeneratedAt, strategyProfile.strategy()
 316:         );
 317:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.MULTI_TIMEFRAME, profileStartedNs);
 318: 
 319:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 320:         BtcMarketContextResult btcContext = btcMarketContextService.evaluate(
 321:                 symbol, i.intervalCode(), confluence.finalDecision(), confluence.entryAllowed(), signalGeneratedAt
 322:         );
 323:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.BTC_CONTEXT, profileStartedNs);
 324: 
 325:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 326:         // FIX-11N: Regression Replay must never issue live Binance futures requests for a
 327:         // historical candle. Keep normal Production and FIX-043 Production recovery exactly
 328:         // on the pre-existing live evaluate(...) path; only analyzeForRegression() selects
 329:         // evaluateHistorical(...). This removes Replay-only network I/O without changing any
 330:         // Production derivatives behavior, thresholds, ordering, wallet logic or execution.
 331:         DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives = regressionReplay
 332:                 ? derivativesPositioningService.evaluateHistorical(
 333:                     symbol, i.intervalCode(), btcContext.finalDecision(), btcContext.entryAllowed(), signalGeneratedAt)
 334:                 : derivativesPositioningService.evaluate(
 335:                     symbol, i.intervalCode(), btcContext.finalDecision(), btcContext.entryAllowed(), signalGeneratedAt);
 336:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.DERIVATIVES, profileStartedNs);
 337: 
 338:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 339:         OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity = historicalReplay
 340:                 ? orderBookLiquidityService.evaluateHistorical(
 341:                     symbol, i.intervalCode(), derivatives.finalDecision(), derivatives.entryAllowed(),
 342:                     i.latestPrice(), atrRisk.stopLoss(), atrRisk.takeProfit(), signalGeneratedAt)
 343:                 : orderBookLiquidityService.evaluate(
 344:                     symbol, i.intervalCode(), derivatives.finalDecision(), derivatives.entryAllowed(),
 345:                     i.latestPrice(), atrRisk.stopLoss(), atrRisk.takeProfit(), signalGeneratedAt);
 346:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.ORDER_BOOK, profileStartedNs);
 347: 
 348:         // FIX-091 / Fix 5: transition authority is evaluated only after all structural/context
 349:         // evidence exists. In Replay it can identify a candidate early, but a RANGE-location
 350:         // exception is only granted when the full BTC/liquidity/HTF/ATR safety set is complete.
 351:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 352:         EntryAuthorityDecision entryAuthority = entryAuthorityService.evaluate(
 353:                 i, regimeState, strategyProfile, strategyScore, trendStructure, atrRisk, confluence, btcContext, liquidity);
 354:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.ENTRY_AUTHORITY, profileStartedNs);
 355: 
 356:         // FIX-042 / FIX-036 + FIX-091: ordinary RANGE behavior remains unchanged. Only a verified,
 357:         // safety-complete TRANSITION_PROBE may use the explicit transition overload.
 358:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 359:         RangeEntryLocationAssessment rangeEntryLocation = rangeEntryLocationService.evaluate(
 360:                 i, strategyProfile, strategyScore, trendStructure, entryAuthority);
 361:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION, profileStartedNs);
 362: 
 363:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 364:         FinalDecisionResult finalDecision = finalDecisionService.decide(
 365:                 baseDecision,
 366:                 atrAdjustedDecision,
 367:                 atrRisk,
 368:                 rangeEntryLocation,
 369:                 strategyProfile,
 370:                 regimeAssessment,
  30:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode="OFF";
  31:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-max-age-seconds:15}") private long maxAge=15;
  32:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value("${local-candle.price-future-skew-seconds:2}") private long futureSkew=2;
  33:     private static final org.slf4j.Logger log=org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(MarketPriceEventService.class);
  34:     private final java.util.Set<Long> inFlight=java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  35:     public static final String DIRECT_SOURCE="BINANCE_KLINE_DIRECT_FIX153";
  36:     public static BigDecimal normalizePrice(BigDecimal price){
  37:         return price==null?null:price.setScale(PRICE_SCALE,java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
  38:     }
  39:     private Instant referenceNow(){Instant local=Instant.now();return exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);}
  40:     public String invalidReason(BigDecimal price,Instant observed,Instant now){
  41:         if(price==null || price.signum()<=0)return "INVALID_PRICE";
  42:         if(observed==null)return "MISSING_TIMESTAMP";
  43:         if(observed.isAfter(now.plusSeconds(futureSkew)))return "FUTURE_TIMESTAMP";
  44:         if(observed.isBefore(now.minusSeconds(maxAge)))return "STALE_PRICE";
  45:         return null;
  46:     }
  47:     /** FIX-153: called in INPUT transaction. Invalid observations never occupy slot 1.
  48:      * A duplicate-key error is classified only if the exact canonical key exists; other
  49:      * duplicate constraints/errors propagate. JDBC statement errors do not poison a JPA proxy TX. */
  50:     public Long recordPending(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observed){
  51:         BigDecimal normalizedPrice=normalizePrice(price);
  52:         Instant now=referenceNow();String invalid=invalidReason(normalizedPrice,observed,now);
  53:         if(invalid!=null){
  54:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
  55:             Long ageMs=observed==null?null:java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis();
  56:             log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_NOT_CANONICAL] symbol={}, price={}, observedUtc={}, observationAgeMs={}, reason={}",symbol,normalizedPrice,observed,ageMs,invalid);
  57:             return null;
  58:         }
  59:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder keys=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
  60:         try {
  61:             jdbcTemplate.update(connection->{
  62:                 var ps=connection.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'PENDING',1)",new String[]{"id"});
  63:                 ps.setString(1,symbol);ps.setTimestamp(2,Timestamp.from(observed));ps.setBigDecimal(3,normalizedPrice);ps.setString(4,DIRECT_SOURCE);return ps;
  64:             },keys);
  65:             long id=java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(keys.getKey(),"generated price id").longValue();inFlight.add(id);return id;
  66:         }catch(org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate){
  67:             var first=jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id,price FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND ordering_slot=1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed));
  68:             if(first.size()!=1)throw duplicate;
  69:             BigDecimal existing=(BigDecimal)first.getFirst().get("price");
  70:             log.warn("[FIX-153][EQUAL_TIMESTAMP_REJECTED] symbol={}, observedUtc={}, firstEvent={}, firstPrice={}, rejectedPrice={}, observationAgeMs={}, priceDisagreement={}",symbol,observed,first.getFirst().get("id"),existing,normalizedPrice,java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis(),existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)!=0);
  71:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,delivery_status,ordering_slot,outcome_reason) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'NON_CANONICAL',NULL,?)",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),normalizedPrice,DIRECT_SOURCE,existing.compareTo(normalizedPrice)==0?"DUPLICATE_TIMESTAMP":"AMBIGUOUS_EQUAL_TIMESTAMP");
  72:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);return null;
  73:         }
  74:     }
  75:     public void release(long id){inFlight.remove(id);}
  76:     /** Runs after existing symbol/position lock at READ_COMMITTED, never counts unresolved receipts. */
  77:     public boolean laterApplied(String symbol,Instant observed){
  78:         return !jdbcTemplate.queryForList("SELECT id FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at>? AND source IN ('BINANCE_KLINE_LIVE_CLOSE',?) AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') ORDER BY observed_at DESC LIMIT 1",symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),DIRECT_SOURCE).isEmpty();
  79:     }
  80:     /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
  81:      * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
  82:     public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
  83:         price=normalizePrice(price);
  84:         if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  85:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");
  86:         if(!java.util.Set.of("APPLIED","NON_CANONICAL").contains(state))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 invalid effects outcome");
  87:         int n=jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=?,outcome_reason=? WHERE id=? AND source=? AND symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND price=? AND ordering_slot=1 AND delivery_status='PENDING'",state,reason,id,DIRECT_SOURCE,symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),price);
  88:         if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);
  89:         org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization(){
  90:             @Override public void afterCompletion(int status){
  91:                 if(status==STATUS_COMMITTED){
  92:                     com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count("APPLIED".equals(state)?com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_APPLIED:com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_REJECTED);
  93:                     if("APPLIED".equals(state))com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.protection(symbol,observed);
  94:                     log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, observedUtc={}, observedKsa={}, outcome={}, reason={}",id,symbol,observed,observed.atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)),state,reason);
  95:                 }else log.debug("[FIX-153][PRICE_OUTCOME_NOT_COMMITTED] event={}, symbol={}, proposedOutcome={}, transactionCompletion={}; durable outcome must be read",id,symbol,state,status);
  96:             }
  97:         });
  98:     }
  99:     /** Startup and periodic conditional resolution: no replay and no symbol-entry fence.
 100:      * The conditional update waits behind an in-flight effect TX and cannot override APPLIED.
 101:      * Runtime absence alone is insufficient; candidates must also exceed a configured bound. */
 102:     @org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled(initialDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-initial-delay-ms:5000}",fixedDelayString="${local-candle.resolver-delay-ms:5000}")
 103:     public void resolvePending(){
 104:         if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
 105:         try {
 106:             // FIX-153: housekeeping uses its own statement timeout, without mutating the shared template.
 107:             var bounded=new JdbcTemplate(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(jdbcTemplate.getDataSource()));bounded.setQueryTimeout(2);
 108:             long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
 109:             var rows=bounded.queryForList("SELECT id,symbol,observed_at FROM market_price_event WHERE source=? AND delivery_status='PENDING' AND created_at<? ORDER BY created_at,id LIMIT 100",DIRECT_SOURCE,Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(120)));

   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }
 197: }
  70: 
  71: ### Out of scope
  72: 
  73: - Any Production strategy change, sizing change, exit change, or wallet change.
  74: - Live-exchange orders, fills, fees, slippage, or order-book execution modelling.
  75: - Replaying database locks, thread scheduling, JVM pauses, WebSocket transport,
  76:   external REST failures, or transaction retries as historical facts.
  77: - FIX-153, FIX-154, and FIX-155 trading-policy changes.
  78: - Deleting Production evidence or existing historical Replay/archive rows.
  79: 
  80: ## 4. Contractual modes
  81: 
  82: | Mode | Purpose | Missing evidence behaviour | Claim allowed |
  83: |---|---|---|---|
  84: | `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` | Reproduce Production rules using an explicitly selected virtual Replay account and complete market/decision evidence. | Refuse the run before execution. | Business-logic parity. |
  85: | `SIMULATION` | Manual what-if/research run. | Continue but persist all approximation reasons. | Simulation only. |
  86: | `EXPERIMENTAL_RESEARCH` | Explicit, separately approved research such as FIX-155. | Per experiment contract. | Never Production parity. |
  87: 
  88: The default Administration Replay action must be `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY`; the UI
  89: must make `SIMULATION` and `EXPERIMENTAL_RESEARCH` explicit opt-in choices.
  90: 
  91: ## 5. Business-logic-parity eligibility
  92: 
  93: A `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` run must fail before generated signals or shadow wallet
  94: state are written unless all of the following hold:
  95: 
  96: 1. Every requested candle identity is closed and within the quality contract.
  97: 2. A complete immutable `ReplayAccountProfile` is supplied: starting virtual
  98:    USDT, reserve, daily budget, daily loss state, maximum-new-position/global
  99:    limits, and wallet settings. It is copied to replay-only tables at run start
 100:    and is never read from or written to a Production wallet table.
 101: 3. The selected replay universe is declared. The virtual account applies
 102:    global limits across every symbol included in that run; no claim is made
 103:    that it reproduces the historical Production wallet's other positions.
 104: 4. The selected profile contains versioned execution constraints for relevant
 105:    symbols, including minimum executable notional. Replay never fetches a
 106:    current exchange constraint while evaluating historical events.
 107: 5. Every canonical direct price observation required from the first replay
 108:    position open through its terminal close is represented by accepted
 109:    `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` evidence, with no unresolved `PENDING` row in the whole
 110:    consumed range.
 111: 6. Required historical order-book evidence exists whenever the feature was
 112:    enabled for the corresponding Production decision.
 113: 7. Required historical derivatives evidence exists whenever derivatives were
 114:    enabled and could change confidence or entry authority. Until durable
 115:    derivatives persistence exists, affected requests must be refused in this
 116:    mode rather than silently treating UNAVAILABLE as equivalent.
 117: 8. The exact Production configuration/version fingerprint is available for the
 118:    requested period, or all version-dependent decision paths are marked
 119:    unavailable and the mode is refused.
 120: 
 121: `SIMULATION` may use configured defaults or fixed starting capital but must
 122: store each deviation in its output. It may never be displayed as parity.
 123: 
 124: ### 5.1 Wallet isolation boundary
 125: 
 126: Replay never reads Production `wallet_asset`, `wallet_settings`,
 127: `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, daily-statistics,
 128: or wallet-snapshot rows as a decision input. The existing data does not retain
 129: complete historical wallet state and is deliberately irrelevant to a Replay
 130: account. A user-selected/versioned `ReplayAccountProfile` is the only account
 131: input. This preserves safe isolation while exercising the same pure sizing and
 132: position-management formulas.
 133: 
 134: The profile also contains symbol execution constraints used by shared
 135: paper-order guards. `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` must be split into a pure
 136: minimum-notional evaluator plus a Production-only exchange-constraint provider;
 137: Replay receives immutable profile values and may not issue Binance HTTP.
 138: 
 139: ## 6. Agreed behaviour changes
 140: 
 141: ### 6.1 Replay starting state
 142: 
 143: Replace hard-coded starting capital with an immutable `ReplayStartingState`.
 144: It is seeded from the selected, versioned `ReplayAccountProfile` and stored
 145: under the replay run ID before processing begins. Shadow wallet/position
 146: services consume only this replay-local state and replay tables.
 147: 
 148: The run-start state must include profile ID/version, creation timestamp, version/config fingerprint,
 149: virtual USDT/asset balances, daily-budget state, daily loss state, virtual
 150: managed positions, virtual paper positions, replay idempotency keys, and a
 151: versioned wallet/settings/configuration fingerprint. It may not be refreshed
 152: from Production while a run is executing.
 153: 
 154: ### 6.2 Shared paper-wallet business authority
 155: 
 156: Extract or complete pure shared policies for:
 157: 
 158: - initial and progressive sizing;
 159: - reserve, daily budget, daily-loss, maximum-new-position, and global
 160:   maximum-open-position controls;
 161: - buy/sell/partial-harvest quantity and cost-basis calculations;
 162: - position average entry, target/stop updates, profit-lock state, and near-TP
 163:   state transitions;
 164: - idempotency-key interpretation.
 165: 
 166: Production retains its current transactional repositories. Replay receives
 167: replay-local adapters implementing the same policy inputs/outputs. No policy
 168: may branch on `replay=true` to produce a different business decision.
 169: 
 170: ### 6.3 Price evidence
 171: 
 172: Replace the boolean/label derived from ƒ?oany event existsƒ?? with a coverage
 173: result containing requested interval, covered interval, gaps, accepted event
 174: count, rejected/unresolved count, and every position-open interval requiring
 175: protection.
 176: 
 177: `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` rejects incomplete coverage. `SIMULATION` may use
 178: signal-price fallback only with the persisted reason `PRICE_PATH_INCOMPLETE`.
 179: 
 180: ### 6.4 Historical decision evidence
 181: 
 182: Persist versioned derivatives and order-book observations required by
 183: Production, or mark affected historical intervals ineligible for business-logic
 184: parity. Replay must use the same normalized evaluator and only evidence at or
 185: before the replay clock. It must never call live Binance endpoints.
 186: 
 187: ### 6.5 Event ordering and time
 188: 
 189: Use canonical UTC `observed_at` and event IDs for price ordering. Define a
 190: single deterministic tie order matching Productionƒ?Ts committed sequence:
 191: 
 192: 1. accepted price protection event;
 193: 2. resulting position/wallet state;
 194: 3. closed-candle analysis signal;
 195: 4. signal-driven position action.
 196: 
 197: If durable evidence cannot establish the required tie order, business-logic parity
 198: must refuse the run. Replay timestamps remain presentation-neutral UTC;
 199: KSA conversion belongs only to UI/reporting.
 200: 
 201: ### 6.6 Retired Replay-only behaviour
 202: 
 203: The following are **candidates for removal**, subject to complete-reference
 204: review and approval. They must be removed from baseline parity runs now; any
 205: future research requires an independently approved module and run mode.
 206: 
 207: | Registry ID | Candidate | Proposed disposition |
 208: |---|---|---|
 209: | RPR-RM-01 | `DefensiveRiskReductionReplayObserver` / FIX-11K | Remove source, controller/UI/output tables only after archive/export compatibility is proved. |
 210: | RPR-RM-02 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` / FIX-11Q | Remove source, controller/UI/output tables only after archive/export compatibility is proved. |
 211: | RPR-RM-03 | pre-FIX-122 replay revision selector | Remove the UI/API option and old comparison path; Production-current FIX-122 remains the only parity rule. |
 212: | RPR-RM-04 | `EXPERIMENTAL` logic mode if no approved user-facing experiment consumes it | Remove after exact reference search; otherwise retain as an isolated framework with no baseline access. |
 213: 
 214: `ReplayDataSource.DATABASE` versus `DATASET` is not retired business logic:
 215: both must remain only if a test proves equal outputs. It is a data-access
 216: implementation choice, not an old Production-rule selector.
 217: 
 218: ## 7. Implementation change registry
 219: 
 220: No implementation item is authorized until this exact revision is approved.
 221: 
 222: | ID | Area | Required change |
 223: |---|---|---|
 224: | RPR-01 | run contract | Add persisted mode, evidence status, account profile/state ID, version/config fingerprint, and refusal reason to the replay run. |
 225: | RPR-02 | replay account schema/service | Add `ReplayAccountProfile` and immutable run-start-state tables. Seed virtual state only from the selected Replay profile. |
 226: | RPR-03 | wallet authority | Extract/reuse pure paper-wallet policy and provide Production/replay persistence adapters; remove direct Replay reads of `WalletSettingsRepository`. |
 227: | RPR-03a | execution constraints | Split minimum-notional evaluation from live exchange constraint acquisition; inject immutable profile constraints into Replay and prohibit Replay Binance HTTP. |
 228: | RPR-04 | shadow replay | Replace fixed capital/local single-position assumptions with replay-local state and multi-symbol/global-limit coordinator. |
 229: | RPR-05 | price evidence | Add interval coverage validator and replace the current any-event exact-price label. |
 230: | RPR-06 | historical context | Add versioned persistence/lookup/qualification for order book and derivatives, or explicit parity refusal. |
 231: | RPR-07 | timing/order | Persist/use deterministic committed event ordering and reject unknown ordering for parity runs. |
 232: | RPR-08 | certificate | Add immutable parity certificate containing field comparisons, evidence counts, deviations, and final claim. |
 233: | RPR-09 | removals | Execute only the approved RPR-RM entries, including migration/archive compatibility. |
 234: | RPR-10 | UI/API | Make mode explicit; default to business-logic parity; display refusal/deviation/certificate details. |
 235: | RPR-11 | tests | Add unit, integration, MySQL, complete scenario, no-look-ahead, and Production/Replay comparison tests below. |
 236: | RPR-12 | docs | Update architecture, rules, changelog, investigation, operational runbook, and parity contract. |
 237: 
 238: ## 8. Error handling, concurrency, and transactions
 239: 
 240: - Replay-state creation is isolated and must be consistent for one profile version.
 241: - Business-logic-parity evidence validation happens before replay execution; no
 242:   partial shadow run may be labelled a parity run.
 243: - Replay output writes are transactionally isolated by `test_run_id`; a failed
 244:   run records its failure and certificate status without changing Production.
 245: - Replay must not lock Production wallet/position rows for business mutation.
 246: - Replay-profile/state reads must not expose credentials and must log only IDs,
 247:   UTC times, symbol counts, mode, and evidence result.
 248: - Cancellation preserves isolated run evidence and cannot leave a completed
 249:   parity certificate.
 250: 
 251: ## 9. Logging and observability registry
 252: 
 253: | ID | Event | Mandatory fields |
 254: |---|---|---|
 255: | RPR-L-01 | virtual starting state created/refused | runId, mode, profile ID/version, virtual balances/position counts, config fingerprint, reason |
 256: | RPR-L-02 | evidence qualified/refused | runId, symbol(s), from/to UTC, candle/price/orderbook/derivatives status, gap counts |
 257: | RPR-L-03 | shadow execution decision | runId, symbol, signal/candle ID, decision code, shared-policy source, replay state ID |
 258: | RPR-L-04 | parity mismatch | runId, field, Production value, replay value, evidence reference, severity |
 259: | RPR-L-05 | certificate completed | runId, claim, matched/mismatched field counts, simulation deviations, duration |
 260: | RPR-L-06 | retired path blocked | runId, removed/experimental feature ID, requested source, outcome |
 261: 
 262: ## 10. Database/configuration/trading impact
 263: 
 264: - **Database:** additive replay account/snapshot/certificate/evidence schema and any
 265:   required durable historical derivatives observations. Applied migrations are
 266:   never edited. Archive migration/retention is mandatory before deletion of
 267:   retired Replay-only output tables.
 268: - **Configuration:** add replay mode/evidence policy only. Production defaults
 269:   and trading configuration remain unchanged.
 270: - **Trading behaviour:** NONE. Production is read-only to this feature and no
 271:   live/paper Production decision, wallet mutation, position, or protection
 272:   rule may change.
 273: - **Replay behaviour:** baseline becomes evidence-qualified business-logic parity
 274:   or explicitly labelled simulation; retired experimental paths are removed or
 275:   isolated only as approved above.
 276: 
 277: ## 11. Test and validation registry
 278: 
 279: | ID | Required proof |
 280: |---|---|
 281: | RPR-T-01 | The same profile inputs produce equal Production/replay sizing, reserve, daily budget, daily-loss, and global-position-limit decisions through shared policies. |
 282: | RPR-T-01a | Replay refuses `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` when no immutable replay account profile is selected; it must never silently use a Production wallet row. |
 283: | RPR-T-02 | A missing, unresolved, rejected, or coverage-gapped price event refuses business-logic parity and labels simulation correctly. |
 284: | RPR-T-03 | Price-event/signal same-time ordering matches Production for TP, SL, profit lock, normal exit, and signal SELL. |
 285: | RPR-T-04 | End-to-end initial BUY, progressive add, target extension, rebase, stop, profit lock, near-TP partial harvest, terminal exit, and P&L lifecycle match field-for-field. |
 286: | RPR-T-05 | Historical order book/derivatives use only as-of persisted data; missing evidence refuses parity; no live HTTP occurs. |
 287: | RPR-T-05a | Replay never reads a Production wallet settings row or calls a Binance minimum-notional endpoint; profile values drive the same pure minimum-order decision. |
 288: | RPR-T-06 | Multi-symbol global wallet/max-position test proves no isolated-symbol bypass. |
 289: | RPR-T-07 | Cancellation/error tests prove Production tables remain unchanged and certificates cannot falsely pass. |
 290: | RPR-T-08 | MySQL migration and transaction tests use a disposable database. |
 291: | RPR-T-09 | Dataset and database replay inputs produce equivalent output for the same qualified evidence, or one path is removed. |
 292: | RPR-T-10 | Removal tests prove baseline Replay cannot invoke FIX-11K, FIX-11Q, or pre-FIX-122 paths. |
 293: | RPR-T-11 | Real historical parity suite compares selected known Production windows and produces stored certificates. |
 294: 
 295: ## 12. Risks, rollback, and acceptance
 296: 
 297: - Risk: historical data required for verified parity may not exist. Mitigation:
 298:   refuse parity and use clearly labelled simulation; do not invent evidence.
 299: - Risk: snapshot schema increases storage. Mitigation: bounded retention and
 300:   archive strategy, never deletion of active/investigation evidence.
 301: - Risk: retiring observers breaks historical UI/archive reads. Mitigation:
 302:   migration and archive/export compatibility tests before removal.
 303: - Rollback: disable new business-logic-parity mode and retain existing archived
 304:   output; Production requires no rollback because it is never mutated.
 305: 
 306: Acceptance requires: all registry IDs reconciled; no unregistered code change;
 307: all test evidence green; disposable MySQL validation; complete source review by
 308: Claude; Codex verification of the exact reviewed revision; and explicit user
 309: authorization after both agents approve this same document.
 310:

  44:         }
  45:     }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.4-DRAFT fails the mandatory contract standard. Its area-level registry omits the required complete file/class/method changes, migrations, configuration keys, transaction/concurrency behavior, and per-artifact master IDs. [AGENTS.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:337) [FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:218)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Replay can reverse the required close-message order. Production performs protection before close-event publication; Replay creates a signal at `candleOpen + interval` but consumes only prices with `observedAt <= generatedAt`. Thus a closing receipt with Binance event time after that boundary is applied after the signal in Replay, though it was protected before signal dispatch in Production. [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:36) [BinanceKlineService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:101) [RegressionTestWorker.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:584) [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:122)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Exact-price Replay omits two Production signal-path effects:
  1. After a live profit-lock hold, Production’s next authoritative signal-price evaluation closes directly with `PROFIT_LOCK`, without re-running the label-based hold policy.
  2. Production runs `PositionManagementService.analyze()` first, which may execute `POSITION_STOP_LOSS`; exact-price Replay does not invoke it.
  
  [PaperTradingService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:113) [PaperTradingService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:176) [PaperTradingService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:452) [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:183)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Replay inputs are not uniformly closed candles. The worker’s normal candle query does not filter `closed`; it iterates such rows, while the indicator lookup silently substitutes the latest prior closed candle. This can produce a later-stamped signal based on a different candle than the timeline item. ## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** RPR-07 must define a durable causal ordering key linking a closed-candle receipt, price-protection outcome, signal admission, and signal processing—not merely UTC timestamp plus event ID.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** RPR-T-04 needs a concrete oracle: identity keys, initial-state relation, compared fields, tolerances/rounding, absent-record rules, and a classification for scheduling-caused divergence. Decision-policy and virtual-account lifecycle parity must be separate claims.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Price qualification should require complete evidence of the Production-applied observation set and unresolved-state handling, not infer uninterrupted market-tick coverage. A WebSocket gap may accurately represent a period when Production had no protection observations; rejecting it as a parity impossibility would be a false refusal. Simulation may still diagnose it.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The proposed removals remain conditional “candidates,” while RPR-09 authorizes their execution. Each API, UI, table, archive/export, and retention outcome requires an explicit decision. [FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:203)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — correction of peer claims:** The shared-market consumer does insert and update `market_price_event`; the assertion that it does not is contradicted by source. Historical-era semantics may still differ and need a defined eligibility policy. [SharedMarketConsumer.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:490) [SharedMarketConsumer.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:516)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — correction of prior concern:** `PriceEvent` drops the SQL ID, but that alone does not prove current same-timestamp order is lost: SQL orders by ID before the timestamp-only stream sort. IDs are nevertheless required to meet the proposed multi-symbol/durable-order contract and to handle legacy ties. [MarketPriceEventService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:118) [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:89)

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, runtime logs, deployed configuration, production system, build, or tests were accessed/run. Frequency of the close-boundary ordering defect and historical evidence completeness are unproven.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** A corrected specification must select either a whole-window preflight or a deterministic, non-persisting dry-run before any replay-output write.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The worktree is materially dirty, so repository source establishes only the inspected working-tree behavior, not deployed behavior.

VERDICT: REJECTED
